```
   142→    def emit_valu_mux_sharded(self, dest, idx, nodes, win, scratch_map, s_mux_tmp):
   143→        # Sharded Mux to use limited registers (v_tmp1, v_addr)
   144→        # Supports Depth 0 (1 node) per shard.
   145→        shard_size = 1
   146→        sorted_nodes = sorted(list(nodes))
   147→
   148→        # Initialize dest (accumulator) to 0
   149→        v_zero = self.get_vconst(0)
   150→        self.emit_op("&", dest, [dest, v_zero])
   151→
   152→        for i in range(0, len(sorted_nodes), shard_size):
   153→            chunk = sorted_nodes[i : i + shard_size]
   154→            res_reg = self.emit_valu_mux_binary_internal(
   155→                idx, chunk, win, scratch_map, s_mux_tmp
   156→            )
   157→            # res_reg is v_addr (scratch). Accumulate.
   158→            self.emit_op("+", dest, [dest, res_reg])
   159→
   160→    def emit_valu_mux_binary_internal(
   161→        self, idx, node_list, win, scratch_map, s_mux_tmp
   162→    ):
   163→        # Internal version returning result_reg
   164→        # Uses independent temps [v_addr, v_tmp1] (passed via win['mux_tmps'])
   165→        temps = win["mux_tmps"]
   166→        v_one = self.get_vconst(1)
   167→
   168→        def recurse(nodes, reg_offset):
   169→            if len(nodes) == 1:
   170→                # Base Case with Equality Check
   171→                node = nodes[0]
   172→                s_addr = scratch_map[node]
   173→                tgt = temps[reg_offset]
   174→
   175→                # Broadcast value (node value)
   176→                self.packer.add_slot(
   177→                    "valu",
   178→                    ("vbroadcast", tgt, s_addr),
   179→                    reads=(s_addr,),
   180→                    writes=tuple(range(tgt, tgt + VLEN)),
   181→                )
   182→
   183→                # Equality Check: (idx < node+1) & !(idx < node)
   184→                # T0 (tgt) = Mask. T1 (scratch) = Temp.
   185→                # Optimization: Use passed s_mux_tmp transient const
   186→                v_scratch = temps[reg_offset + 1]
   187→
   188→                # T0 = idx < node+1
   189→                # Load node+1 const
   190→                self.packer.add_slot(
   191→                    "load", ("const", s_mux_tmp, node + 1), writes=(s_mux_tmp,)
   192→                )
   193→                # Broadcast node+1 to T1 (v_scratch)
   194→                self.packer.add_slot(
   195→                    "valu",
   196→                    ("vbroadcast", v_scratch, s_mux_tmp),
   197→                    reads=(s_mux_tmp,),
   198→                    writes=tuple(range(v_scratch, v_scratch + VLEN)),
   199→                )
   200→                # Compare T0 = idx < T1
   201→                self.emit_op("<", tgt, [idx, v_scratch])
   202→
   203→                # T1 = idx < node
   204→                # Load node const
   205→                self.packer.add_slot(
   206→                    "load", ("const", s_mux_tmp, node), writes=(s_mux_tmp,)
   207→                )
   208→                # Broadcast node to T1 (v_scratch)
   209→                self.packer.add_slot(
   210→                    "valu",
   211→                    ("vbroadcast", v_scratch, s_mux_tmp),
   212→                    reads=(s_mux_tmp,),
   213→                    writes=tuple(range(v_scratch, v_scratch + VLEN)),
   214→                )
   215→                # Compare T1 = idx < T1
   216→                self.emit_op("<", v_scratch, [idx, v_scratch])
   217→
   218→                # T1 = !T1
   219→                self.emit_op("^", v_scratch, [v_scratch, v_one])
   220→
   221→                # T0 = T0 & T1
   222→                self.emit_op("&", tgt, [tgt, v_scratch])
   223→
   224→                # Load Value into T1
   225→                self.packer.add_slot(
   226→                    "valu",
   227→                    ("vbroadcast", v_scratch, s_addr),
   228→                    reads=(s_addr,),
   229→                    writes=tuple(range(v_scratch, v_scratch + VLEN)),
   230→                )
   231→
   232→                # Select: tgt = (Mask) ? T1 : 0
   233→                v_zero = self.get_vconst(0)
   234→                self.packer.add_slot(
   235→                    "flow",
   236→                    ("vselect", tgt, tgt, v_scratch, v_zero),
   237→                    reads=tuple(range(tgt, tgt + VLEN))
   238→                    + tuple(range(v_scratch, v_scratch + VLEN))
   239→                    + tuple(range(v_zero, v_zero + VLEN)),
   240→                    writes=tuple(range(tgt, tgt + VLEN)),
   241→                )
   242→                return tgt
   243→
   244→            # Recursive Step (Standard Binary Mux)
   245→            mid = len(nodes) // 2
   246→            left_nodes = nodes[:mid]
   247→            right_nodes = nodes[mid:]
   248→
   249→            r_left = recurse(left_nodes, reg_offset)
   250→            r_right = recurse(right_nodes, reg_offset + 1)
   251→
   252→            # The split logic:
   253→            split_idx = right_nodes[0]
   254→
   255→            # Optimization: load const split_idx into s_mux_tmp
   256→            self.packer.add_slot(
   257→                "load", ("const", s_mux_tmp, split_idx), writes=(s_mux_tmp,)
   258→            )
   259→            # Broadcast to v_cmp (temps[reg_offset+2])
   260→            v_cmp = temps[reg_offset + 2]
   261→            self.packer.add_slot(
   262→                "valu",
   263→                ("vbroadcast", v_cmp, s_mux_tmp),

   267→
   268→            self.emit_op("<", v_cmp, [idx, v_cmp])

   270→            # If idx < split_idx (TRUE) -> Left. Else Right.

   272→                "flow",
   273→                ("vselect", r_left, v_cmp, r_left, r_right),
   274→                reads=tuple(range(v_cmp, v_cmp + VLEN))
   275→                + tuple(range(r_left, r_left + VLEN))
   276→                + tuple(range(r_right, r_right + VLEN)),
   277→                writes=tuple(range(r_left, r_left + VLEN)),
   278→            )
   279→
   280→            return r_left
   281→
   282→        return recurse(node_list, 0)

   284→    def precipitate_crown_cache(self, p_forest_base, rounds):
   285→        """Fabric Alpha: Load the tree crown (R0-R8) into SCALAR scratch only."""
   286→        node_cache = {}  # val -> scratch_addr
   287→
   288→        # Calculate nodes in the crown (up to crown_depth)
   289→        crown_nodes = set()
   290→        current = {0}
   291→        for r in range(min(rounds, self.config.crown_depth)):
   292→            crown_nodes.update(current)
   293→            next_lvl = set()
   294→            for n in current:
   295→                next_lvl.add(n * 2 + 1)
   296→                next_lvl.add(n * 2 + 2)
   297→            current = next_lvl
   298→
   299→        # Load nodes into SCALAR scratch
   300→        for node_idx in sorted(list(crown_nodes)):
   301→            s_addr = self.alloc(f"crown_{node_idx}")
   302→            s_const = self.get_const(node_idx)
   303→            self.packer.add_slot(
   304→                "alu",
   305→                ("+", s_addr, p_forest_base, s_const),
   306→                reads=(p_forest_base, s_const),
   307→                writes=(s_addr,),
   308→            )
   309→            self.packer.add_slot(
   310→                "load",
   311→                ("load", s_addr, s_addr),
   312→                reads=(s_addr,),
   313→                writes=(s_addr,),
   314→                is_mem_read=True,
   315→            )
   316→            node_cache[node_idx] = s_addr
   317→        return node_cache, None  # No v_regs returned
   318→
   319→    def add_hash_hybrid(self, v_val, v_tmp1, v_tmp2, hash_stages):
   320→        for i_stage, (op1, val1, op2, op3, val3) in enumerate(hash_stages):
   321→            v_val1 = self.get_vconst(val1)
   322→            if (
   323→                i_stage == 0
   324→                and not self.config.disable_hash_opt
   325→                and op1 == "+"
   326→                and op2 == "+"
   327→                and op3 == "<<"
   328→            ):
   329→                v_factor = self.get_vconst(1 + (1 << val3))
   330→                self.emit_op("multiply_add", v_val, [v_val, v_factor, v_val1])
   331→            else:
   332→                v_val3 = self.get_vconst(val3)
   333→                self.emit_op(op1, v_tmp1, [v_val, v_val1])
   334→                self.emit_op(op3, v_tmp2, [v_val, v_val3])
   335→                self.emit_op(op2, v_val, [v_tmp1, v_tmp2])
   336→
   337→    def build_kernel(self, forest_height, n_nodes, batch_size, rounds, hash_stages):
   338→        # Quadrature Nexus Optimization: N_VEC=28, Latent Round Folding, Non-Speculative
   339→        N_VEC = 28
   340→        n_windows = N_VEC
   341→        windows = []
   342→        for w in range(n_windows):
   343→            win = {}
   344→            win["v_idx"] = self.valloc(f"v_idx_{w}")
   345→            win["v_val"] = self.valloc(f"v_val_{w}")  # Accumulator/Hash Val
   346→            win["v_node_val"] = self.valloc(f"v_node_val_{w}")
   347→            win["v_tmp1"] = self.valloc(f"v_tmp1_{w}")
   348→            win["v_addr"] = self.valloc(f"v_addr_{w}")
   349→            win["s_mux_tmp"] = self.alloc(f"s_mux_tmp_{w}")
   350→            windows.append(win)
   351→
   352→        # Global Constants
   353→        s_n_nodes = self.alloc("s_n_nodes")
   354→        s_batch_size = self.alloc("s_batch_size")
   355→        p_forest_base = self.alloc("p_forest_base")
   356→        p_idx_base = self.alloc("p_idx_base")
   357→        p_val_base = self.alloc("p_val_base")
   358→
   359→        for i, addr in enumerate(
   360→            [None, s_n_nodes, s_batch_size, None, p_forest_base, p_idx_base, p_val_base]
   361→        ):
   362→            if addr is not None:
   363→                tmp_c = self.get_const(i)
   364→                self.packer.add_slot(
   365→                    "load",
   366→                    ("load", addr, tmp_c),
   367→                    reads=(tmp_c,),
   368→                    writes=(addr,),
   369→                    is_mem_read=True,
   370→                )
   371→
   372→        # Safe Optimization: Hoist Node 0 (Root)
   373→        s_root_node = self.alloc("s_root_node")
   374→        s_zero = self.get_const(0)
   375→        self.packer.add_slot(
   376→            "alu",
   377→            ("+", s_root_node, p_forest_base, s_zero),
   378→            reads=(p_forest_base, s_zero),
   379→            writes=(s_root_node,),
   380→        )
   381→        self.packer.add_slot(
   382→            "load",
   383→            ("load", s_root_node, s_root_node),
   384→            reads=(s_root_node,),
   385→            writes=(s_root_node,),
   386→            is_mem_read=True,
   387→        )
   388→
   389→        v_n_nodes = self.valloc("v_n_nodes")
   390→        self.packer.add_slot(
   391→            "valu",
   392→            ("vbroadcast", v_n_nodes, s_n_nodes),
   393→            reads=(s_n_nodes,),
   394→            writes=tuple(range(v_n_nodes, v_n_nodes + VLEN)),
   395→        )
   396→
   397→        v_forest_base = self.valloc("v_forest_base")
   398→        self.packer.add_slot(
   399→            "valu",
   400→            ("vbroadcast", v_forest_base, p_forest_base),
   401→            reads=(p_forest_base,),
   402→            writes=tuple(range(v_forest_base, v_forest_base + VLEN)),
   403→        )
   404→
   405→        g_vzero = self.get_vconst(0)
   406→        g_vone = self.get_vconst(1)
   407→        g_vtwo = self.get_vconst(2)
   408→
   409→        # Helper for batch calc
   410→        vi_tmp_addrs = [self.alloc(f"vi_tmp_addr_{i}") for i in range(1)]
   411→
   412→        batch_stride = VLEN
   413→        core_batch_size = batch_size // N_CORES
   414→        n_core_batches = core_batch_size // VLEN
   415→
   416→        s_core_id = self.alloc("s_core_id")
   417→        self.packer.add_slot("flow", ("coreid", s_core_id), writes=(s_core_id,))
   418→        s_core_batch = self.get_const(core_batch_size)
   419→        s_core_offset = self.alloc("s_core_offset")
   420→        self.packer.add_slot(
   421→            "alu",
   422→            ("*", s_core_offset, s_core_id, s_core_batch),
   423→            reads=(s_core_id, s_core_batch),
   424→            writes=(s_core_offset,),
   425→        )
   426→        self.packer.barrier()
   427→
   428→        # Calculate Start Pointers (SSA style)
   429→        p_idx_start = self.alloc("p_idx_start")
   430→        p_val_start = self.alloc("p_val_start")
   431→        self.packer.add_slot(
   432→            "alu",
   433→            ("+", p_idx_start, p_idx_base, s_core_offset),
   434→            reads=(p_idx_base, s_core_offset),
   435→            writes=(p_idx_start,),
   436→        )
   437→        self.packer.add_slot(
   438→            "alu",
   439→            ("+", p_val_start, p_val_base, s_core_offset),
   440→            reads=(p_val_base, s_core_offset),
   441→            writes=(p_val_start,),
   442→        )
   443→        self.packer.barrier()
   444→
   445→        # Main Loop: Process Chunks of N_VEC vectors
   446→        for b_start in range(0, n_core_batches, n_windows):
   447→            b_end = min(b_start + n_windows, n_core_batches)
   448→            chunk_size = b_end - b_start
   449→
   450→            # Initial Loads
   451→            for i in range(chunk_size):
   452→                win = windows[i]
   453→                s_offset = self.get_const((b_start + i) * batch_stride)
   454→                s_tmp_addr = vi_tmp_addrs[0]
   455→
   456→                # Load Initial Index -> v_idx
   457→                self.packer.add_slot(
   458→                    "alu",
   459→                    ("+", s_tmp_addr, p_idx_start, s_offset),
   460→                    reads=(p_idx_start, s_offset),
   461→                    writes=(s_tmp_addr,),
   462→                )
   463→                self.packer.add_slot(
   464→                    "load",
   465→                    ("vload", win["v_idx"], s_tmp_addr),
   466→                    reads=(s_tmp_addr,),
   467→                    writes=tuple(range(win["v_idx"], win["v_idx"] + VLEN)),
   468→                    is_mem_read=True,
   469→                )
   470→
   471→                # Load Initial Value -> v_val
   472→                self.packer.add_slot(
   473→                    "alu",
   474→                    ("+", s_tmp_addr, p_val_start, s_offset),
   475→                    reads=(p_val_start, s_offset),
   476→                    writes=(s_tmp_addr,),
   477→                )
   478→                self.packer.add_slot(
   479→                    "load",
   480→                    ("vload", win["v_val"], s_tmp_addr),
   481→                    reads=(s_tmp_addr,),
   482→                    writes=tuple(range(win["v_val"], win["v_val"] + VLEN)),
   483→                    is_mem_read=True,
   484→                )
   485→
   486→            self.packer.barrier()
   487→
   488→            # Pipelined Computation Logic
   489→            for r in range(rounds):
   490→                for i in range(chunk_size):
   491→                    win = windows[i]
   492→
   493→                    if r == 0:
   494→                        # Round 0: Constant Broadcast
   495→                        self.packer.add_slot(
   496→                            "valu",
   497→                            ("vbroadcast", win["v_node_val"], s_root_node),
   498→                            reads=(s_root_node,),
   499→                            writes=tuple(
   500→                                range(win["v_node_val"], win["v_node_val"] + VLEN)
   501→                            ),
   502→                        )
   503→                    else:
   504→                        # 1. Calc Address (Base + idx)
   505→                        self.emit_op("+", win["v_addr"], [v_forest_base, win["v_idx"]])
   506→
   507→                        # 2. Load Node (Correct Gather)
   508→                        for lane in range(VLEN):
   509→                            self.packer.add_slot(
   510→                                "load",
   511→                                (
   512→                                    "load",
   513→                                    win["v_node_val"] + lane,
   514→                                    win["v_addr"] + lane,
   515→                                ),
   516→                                reads=(win["v_addr"] + lane,),
   517→                                writes=(win["v_node_val"] + lane,),
   518→                                is_mem_read=True,
   519→                            )
   520→
   521→                    # 3. Hash
   522→                    self.emit_op("^", win["v_val"], [win["v_val"], win["v_node_val"]])
   523→                    self.add_hash_hybrid(
   524→                        win["v_val"], win["v_tmp1"], win["v_addr"], hash_stages
   525→                    )
   526→
   527→                    # 4. Update Index
   528→                    self.emit_op("&", win["v_tmp1"], [win["v_val"], g_vone])
   529→                    self.emit_op(
   530→                        "multiply_add", win["v_idx"], [win["v_idx"], g_vtwo, g_vone]
   531→                    )
   532→                    self.emit_op("+", win["v_idx"], [win["v_idx"], win["v_tmp1"]])
   533→
   534→                    # Wrap (Correct vselect logic)
   535→                    # reuse v_tmp1 for condition (idx < n_nodes)
   536→                    self.emit_op("<", win["v_tmp1"], [win["v_idx"], v_n_nodes])
   537→
   538→                    # select: idx = (v_tmp1) ? idx : 0
   539→                    self.packer.add_slot(
   540→                        "flow",
   541→                        ("vselect", win["v_idx"], win["v_tmp1"], win["v_idx"], g_vzero),
   542→                        reads=tuple(range(win["v_tmp1"], win["v_tmp1"] + VLEN))
   543→                        + tuple(range(win["v_idx"], win["v_idx"] + VLEN))
   544→                        + tuple(range(g_vzero, g_vzero + VLEN)),
   545→                        writes=tuple(range(win["v_idx"], win["v_idx"] + VLEN)),
   546→                    )
   547→
   548→            self.packer.barrier()
   549→
   550→            # Writeback
   551→            for i in range(chunk_size):
   552→                win = windows[i]
   553→                s_offset = self.get_const((b_start + i) * batch_stride)
   554→                s_tmp_addr = vi_tmp_addrs[0]
   555→
   556→                # Write Index
   557→                self.packer.add_slot(
   558→                    "alu",
   559→                    ("+", s_tmp_addr, p_idx_start, s_offset),
   560→                    reads=(p_idx_start, s_offset),
   561→                    writes=(s_tmp_addr,),
   562→                )
   563→                self.packer.add_slot(
   564→                    "store",
   565→                    ("vstore", s_tmp_addr, win["v_idx"]),
   566→                    reads=(s_tmp_addr,)
   567→                    + tuple(range(win["v_idx"], win["v_idx"] + VLEN)),
   568→                    is_mem_write=True,
   569→                )
   570→
   571→                # Write Value
   572→                self.packer.add_slot(
   573→                    "alu",
   574→                    ("+", s_tmp_addr, p_val_start, s_offset),
   575→                    reads=(p_val_start, s_offset),
   576→                    writes=(s_tmp_addr,),
   577→                )
   578→                self.packer.add_slot(
   579→                    "store",
   580→                    ("vstore", s_tmp_addr, win["v_val"]),
   581→                    reads=(s_tmp_addr,)
   582→                    + tuple(range(win["v_val"], win["v_val"] + VLEN)),
   583→                    is_mem_write=True,
   584→                )
   585→
   586→        return self.packer.get_instrs()
   587→
```

```
     1→"""
     2→Read the top of perf_takehome.py for more introduction.
     3→
     4→This file is separate mostly for ease of copying it to freeze the machine and
     5→reference kernel for testing.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→import random
     9→from copy import copy
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→from enum import Enum
    12→from typing import Any, Literal
    13→
    14→Engine = Literal["alu", "load", "store", "flow"]
    15→Instruction = dict[Engine, list[tuple]]
    16→
    17→
    18→class CoreState(Enum):
    19→    RUNNING = 1
    20→    PAUSED = 2
    21→    STOPPED = 3
    22→
    23→
    24→@dataclass
    25→class Core:
    26→    id: int
    27→    scratch: list[int]
    28→    trace_buf: list[int]
    29→    pc: int = 0
    30→    state: CoreState = CoreState.RUNNING
    31→
    32→
    33→@dataclass
    34→class DebugInfo:
    35→    """
    36→    We give you some debug info but it's up to you to use it in Machine if you
    37→    want to. You're also welcome to add more.
    38→    """
    39→
    40→    # Maps scratch variable addr to (name, len) pair
    41→    scratch_map: dict[int, (str, int)]
    42→
    43→
    44→def cdiv(a, b):
    45→    return (a + b - 1) // b
    46→
    47→
    48→SLOT_LIMITS = {
    49→    "alu": 12,
    50→    "valu": 6,
    51→    "load": 2,
    52→    "store": 2,
    53→    "flow": 1,
    54→    "debug": 64,
    55→}
    56→
    57→VLEN = 8
    58→# Older versions of the take-home used multiple cores, but this version only uses 1
    59→N_CORES = 32
    60→SCRATCH_SIZE = 1536
    61→BASE_ADDR_TID = 100000
    62→
    63→
    64→class Machine:
    65→    """
    66→    Simulator for a custom VLIW SIMD architecture.
    67→
    68→    VLIW (Very Large Instruction Word): Cores are composed of different
    69→    "engines" each of which can execute multiple "slots" per cycle in parallel.
    70→    How many slots each engine can execute per cycle is limited by SLOT_LIMITS.
    71→    Effects of instructions don't take effect until the end of cycle. Each
    72→    cycle, all engines execute all of their filled slots for that instruction.
    73→    Effects like writes to memory take place after all the inputs are read.
    74→
    75→    SIMD: There are instructions for acting on vectors of VLEN elements in a
    76→    single slot. You can use vload and vstore to load multiple contiguous
    77→    elements but not non-contiguous elements. Use vbroadcast to broadcast a
    78→    scalar to a vector and then operate on vectors with valu instructions.
    79→
    80→    The memory and scratch space are composed of 32-bit words. The solution is
    81→    plucked out of the memory at the end of the program. You can think of the
    82→    scratch space as serving the purpose of registers, constant memory, and a
    83→    manually-managed cache.
    84→
    85→    Here's an example of what an instruction might look like:
    86→
    87→    {"valu": [("*", 4, 0, 0), ("+", 8, 4, 0)], "load": [("load", 16, 17)]}
    88→
    89→    In general every number in an instruction is a scratch address except for
    90→    const and jump, and except for store and some flow instructions the first
    91→    operand is the destination.
    92→
    93→    This comment is not meant to be full ISA documentation though, for the rest
    94→    you should look through the simulator code.
    95→    """
    96→
    97→    def __init__(
    98→        self,
    99→        mem_dump: list[int],
   100→        program: list[Instruction],
   101→        debug_info: DebugInfo,
   102→        n_cores: int = 1,
   103→        scratch_size: int = SCRATCH_SIZE,
   104→        trace: bool = False,
   105→        value_trace: dict[Any, int] = {},
   106→    ):
   107→        self.cores = [
   108→            Core(id=i, scratch=[0] * scratch_size, trace_buf=[]) for i in range(n_cores)
   109→        ]
   110→        self.mem = copy(mem_dump)
   111→        self.program = program
   112→        self.debug_info = debug_info
   113→        self.value_trace = value_trace
   114→        self.prints = False
   115→        self.cycle = 0
   116→        self.enable_pause = True
   117→        self.enable_debug = True
   118→        if trace:
   119→            self.setup_trace()
   120→        else:
   121→            self.trace = None
   122→
   123→    def rewrite_instr(self, instr):
   124→        """
   125→        Rewrite an instruction to use scratch addresses instead of names
   126→        """
   127→        res = {}
   128→        for name, slots in instr.items():
   129→            res[name] = []
   130→            for slot in slots:
   131→                res[name].append(self.rewrite_slot(slot))
   132→        return res
   133→
   134→    def print_step(self, instr, core):
   135→        # print(core.id)
   136→        # print(core.trace_buf)
   137→        print(self.scratch_map(core))
   138→        print(core.pc, instr, self.rewrite_instr(instr))
   139→
   140→    def scratch_map(self, core):
   141→        res = {}
   142→        for addr, (name, length) in self.debug_info.scratch_map.items():
   143→            res[name] = core.scratch[addr : addr + length]
   144→        return res
   145→
   146→    def rewrite_slot(self, slot):
   147→        return tuple(
   148→            self.debug_info.scratch_map.get(s, (None, None))[0] or s for s in slot
   149→        )
   150→
   151→    def setup_trace(self):
   152→        """
   153→        The simulator generates traces in Chrome's Trace Event Format for
   154→        visualization in Perfetto (or chrome://tracing if you prefer it). See
   155→        the bottom of the file for info about how to use this.
   156→
   157→        See the format docs in case you want to add more info to the trace:
   158→        https://docs.google.com/document/d/REDACTED/preview
   159→        """
   160→        self.trace = open("trace.json", "w")
   161→        self.trace.write("[")
   162→        tid_counter = 0
   163→        self.tids = {}
   164→        for ci, core in enumerate(self.cores):
   165→            self.trace.write(
   166→                f'{{"name": "process_name", "ph": "M", "pid": {ci}, "tid": 0, "args": {{"name":"Core {ci}"}}}},\n'
   167→            )
   168→            for name, limit in SLOT_LIMITS.items():
   169→                if name == "debug":
   170→                    continue
   171→                for i in range(limit):
   172→                    tid_counter += 1
   173→                    self.trace.write(
   174→                        f'{{"name": "thread_name", "ph": "M", "pid": {ci}, "tid": {tid_counter}, "args": {{"name":"{name}-{i}"}}}},\n'
   175→                    )
   176→                    self.tids[(ci, name, i)] = tid_counter
   177→
   178→        # Add zero-length events at the start so all slots show up in Perfetto
   179→        for ci, core in enumerate(self.cores):
   180→            for name, limit in SLOT_LIMITS.items():
   181→                if name == "debug":
   182→                    continue
   183→                for i in range(limit):
   184→                    tid = self.tids[(ci, name, i)]
   185→                    self.trace.write(
   186→                        f'{{"name": "init", "cat": "op", "ph": "X", "pid": {ci}, "tid": {tid}, "ts": 0, "dur": 0}},\n'
   187→                    )
   188→        for ci, core in enumerate(self.cores):
   189→            self.trace.write(
   190→                f'{{"name": "process_name", "ph": "M", "pid": {len(self.cores) + ci}, "tid": 0, "args": {{"name":"Core {ci} Scratch"}}}},\n'
   191→            )
   192→            for addr, (name, length) in self.debug_info.scratch_map.items():
   193→                self.trace.write(
   194→                    f'{{"name": "thread_name", "ph": "M", "pid": {len(self.cores) + ci}, "tid": {BASE_ADDR_TID + addr}, "args": {{"name":"{name}-{length}"}}}},\n'
   195→                )
   196→
   197→    def run(self):
   198→        for core in self.cores:
   199→            if core.state == CoreState.PAUSED:
   200→                core.state = CoreState.RUNNING
   201→        while any(c.state == CoreState.RUNNING for c in self.cores):
   202→            has_non_debug = False
   203→            for core in self.cores:
   204→                if core.state != CoreState.RUNNING:
   205→                    continue
   206→                if core.pc >= len(self.program):
   207→                    core.state = CoreState.STOPPED
   208→                    continue
   209→                instr = self.program[core.pc]
   210→                if self.prints:
   211→                    self.print_step(instr, core)
   212→                core.pc += 1
   213→                self.step(instr, core)
   214→                if any(name != "debug" for name in instr.keys()):
   215→                    has_non_debug = True
   216→            if has_non_debug:
   217→                self.cycle += 1
   218→
   219→    def alu(self, core, op, dest, a1, a2):
   220→        a1 = core.scratch[a1]
   221→        a2 = core.scratch[a2]
   222→        match op:
   223→            case "+":
   224→                res = a1 + a2
   225→            case "-":
   226→                res = a1 - a2
   227→            case "*":
   228→                res = a1 * a2
   229→            case "//":
   230→                res = a1 // a2
   231→            case "cdiv":
   232→                res = cdiv(a1, a2)
   233→            case "^":
   234→                res = a1 ^ a2
   235→            case "&":
   236→                res = a1 & a2
   237→            case "|":
   238→                res = a1 | a2
   239→            case "<<":
   240→                res = a1 << a2
   241→            case ">>":
   242→                res = a1 >> a2
   243→            case "%":
   244→                res = a1 % a2
   245→            case "<":
   246→                res = int(a1 < a2)
   247→            case "==":
   248→                res = int(a1 == a2)
   249→            case _:
   250→                raise NotImplementedError(f"Unknown alu op {op}")
   251→        res = res % (2**32)
   252→        self.scratch_write[dest] = res
   253→
   254→    def valu(self, core, *slot):
   255→        match slot:
   256→            case ("vbroadcast", dest, src):
   257→                for i in range(VLEN):
   258→                    self.scratch_write[dest + i] = core.scratch[src]
   259→            case ("multiply_add", dest, a, b, c):
   260→                for i in range(VLEN):
   261→                    mul = (core.scratch[a + i] * core.scratch[b + i]) % (2**32)
   262→                    self.scratch_write[dest + i] = (mul + core.scratch[c + i]) % (2**32)
   263→            case (op, dest, a1, a2):
   264→                for i in range(VLEN):
   265→                    self.alu(core, op, dest + i, a1 + i, a2 + i)
   266→            case _:
   267→                raise NotImplementedError(f"Unknown valu op {slot}")
   268→
   269→    def load(self, core, *slot):
   270→        match slot:
   271→            case ("load", dest, addr):
   272→                # print(dest, addr, core.scratch[addr])
   273→                try:
   274→                    self.scratch_write[dest] = self.mem[core.scratch[addr]]
   275→                except IndexError:
   276→                    scratch_map = self.debug_info.scratch_map if self.debug_info else {}
   277→                    reg_name = scratch_map.get(addr, ("UNKNOWN", 0))[0]
   278→                    print(
   279→                        f"ERROR: load out of bounds. cycle={self.cycle} core={core.id} addr_reg={addr}({reg_name}) val={core.scratch[addr]} len(mem)={len(self.mem)} trace={core.trace_buf}"
   280→                    )
   281→                    raise
   282→            case ("load_offset", dest, addr, offset):
   283→                # Handy for treating vector dest and addr as a full block in the mini-compiler if you want
   284→                self.scratch_write[dest + offset] = self.mem[
   285→                    core.scratch[addr + offset]
   286→                ]
   287→            case ("vload", dest, addr):  # addr is a scalar
   288→                addr = core.scratch[addr]
   289→                for vi in range(VLEN):
   290→                    try:
   291→                        self.scratch_write[dest + vi] = self.mem[addr + vi]
   292→                    except IndexError:
   293→                        print(
   294→                            f"ERROR: vload out of bounds. cycle={self.cycle} core={core.id} addr={addr} vi={vi} len(mem)={len(self.mem)}"
   295→                        )
   296→                        raise
   297→            case ("const", dest, val):
   298→                self.scratch_write[dest] = (val) % (2**32)
   299→            case _:
   300→                raise NotImplementedError(f"Unknown load op {slot}")
   301→
   302→    def store(self, core, *slot):
   303→        match slot:
   304→            case ("store", addr, src):
   305→                addr = core.scratch[addr]
   306→                self.mem_write[addr] = core.scratch[src]
   307→            case ("vstore", addr, src):  # addr is a scalar
   308→                addr = core.scratch[addr]
   309→                for vi in range(VLEN):
   310→                    self.mem_write[addr + vi] = core.scratch[src + vi]
   311→            case _:
   312→                raise NotImplementedError(f"Unknown store op {slot}")
   313→
   314→    def flow(self, core, *slot):
   315→        match slot:
   316→            case ("select", dest, cond, a, b):
   317→                self.scratch_write[dest] = (
   318→                    core.scratch[a] if core.scratch[cond] != 0 else core.scratch[b]
   319→                )
   320→            case ("add_imm", dest, a, imm):
   321→                self.scratch_write[dest] = (core.scratch[a] + imm) % (2**32)
   322→            case ("vselect", dest, cond, a, b):
   323→                for vi in range(VLEN):
   324→                    self.scratch_write[dest + vi] = (
   325→                        core.scratch[a + vi]
   326→                        if core.scratch[cond + vi] != 0
   327→                        else core.scratch[b + vi]
   328→                    )
   329→            case ("halt",):
   330→                core.state = CoreState.STOPPED
   331→            case ("pause",):
   332→                if self.enable_pause:
   333→                    core.state = CoreState.PAUSED
   334→            case ("trace_write", val):
   335→                core.trace_buf.append(core.scratch[val])
   336→            case ("cond_jump", cond, addr):
   337→                if core.scratch[cond] != 0:
   338→                    core.pc = addr
   339→            case ("cond_jump_rel", cond, offset):
   340→                if core.scratch[cond] != 0:
   341→                    core.pc += offset
   342→            case ("jump", addr):
   343→                core.pc = addr
   344→            case ("jump_indirect", addr):
   345→                core.pc = core.scratch[addr]
   346→            case ("coreid", dest):
   347→                self.scratch_write[dest] = core.id
   348→            case _:
   349→                raise NotImplementedError(f"Unknown flow op {slot}")
   350→
   351→    def trace_post_step(self, instr, core):
   352→        # You can add extra stuff to the trace if you want!
   353→        for addr, (name, length) in self.debug_info.scratch_map.items():
   354→            if any((addr + vi) in self.scratch_write for vi in range(length)):
   355→                val = str(core.scratch[addr : addr + length])
   356→                val = val.replace("[", "").replace("]", "")
   357→                self.trace.write(
   358→                    f'{{"name": "{val}", "cat": "op", "ph": "X", "pid": {len(self.cores) + core.id}, "tid": {BASE_ADDR_TID + addr}, "ts": {self.cycle}, "dur": 1 }},\n'
   359→                )
   360→
   361→    def trace_slot(self, core, slot, name, i):
   362→        self.trace.write(
   363→            f'{{"name": "{slot[0]}", "cat": "op", "ph": "X", "pid": {core.id}, "tid": {self.tids[(core.id, name, i)]}, "ts": {self.cycle}, "dur": 1, "args":{{"slot": "{str(slot)}", "named": "{str(self.rewrite_slot(slot))}" }} }},\n'
   364→        )
   365→
   366→    def step(self, instr: Instruction, core):
   367→        """
   368→        Execute all the slots in each engine for a single instruction bundle
   369→        """
   370→        ENGINE_FNS = {
   371→            "alu": self.alu,
   372→            "valu": self.valu,
   373→            "load": self.load,
   374→            "store": self.store,
   375→            "flow": self.flow,
   376→        }
   377→        self.scratch_write = {}
   378→        self.mem_write = {}
   379→        for name, slots in instr.items():
   380→            if name == "debug":
   381→                if not self.enable_debug:
   382→                    continue
   383→                for slot in slots:
   384→                    if slot[0] == "compare":
   385→                        loc, key = slot[1], slot[2]
   386→                        ref = self.value_trace[key]
   387→                        res = core.scratch[loc]
   388→                        assert res == ref, f"{res} != {ref} for {key} at pc={core.pc}"
   389→                    elif slot[0] == "vcompare":
   390→                        loc, keys = slot[1], slot[2]
   391→                        ref = [self.value_trace[key] for key in keys]
   392→                        res = core.scratch[loc : loc + VLEN]
   393→                        assert res == ref, (
   394→                            f"{res} != {ref} for {keys} at pc={core.pc} loc={loc}"
   395→                        )
   396→                continue
   397→            assert len(slots) <= SLOT_LIMITS[name]
   398→            for i, slot in enumerate(slots):
   399→                if self.trace is not None:
   400→                    self.trace_slot(core, slot, name, i)
   401→                ENGINE_FNS[name](core, *slot)
   402→        for addr, val in self.scratch_write.items():
   403→            core.scratch[addr] = val
   404→        for addr, val in self.mem_write.items():
   405→            self.mem[addr] = val
   406→
   407→        if self.trace:
   408→            self.trace_post_step(instr, core)
   409→
   410→        del self.scratch_write
   411→        del self.mem_write
   412→
   413→    def __del__(self):
   414→        if self.trace is not None:
   415→            self.trace.write("]")
   416→            self.trace.close()
   417→
   418→
   419→@dataclass
   420→class Tree:
   421→    """
   422→    An implicit perfect balanced binary tree with values on the nodes.
   423→    """
   424→
   425→    height: int
   426→    values: list[int]
   427→
   428→    @staticmethod
   429→    def generate(height: int):
   430→        n_nodes = 2 ** (height + 1) - 1
   431→        values = [random.randint(0, 2**30 - 1) for _ in range(n_nodes)]
   432→        return Tree(height, values)
   433→
   434→
   435→@dataclass
   436→class Input:
   437→    """
   438→    A batch of inputs, indices to nodes (starting as 0) and initial input
   439→    values. We then iterate these for a specified number of rounds.
   440→    """
   441→
   442→    indices: list[int]
   443→    values: list[int]
   444→    rounds: int
   445→
   446→    @staticmethod
   447→    def generate(forest: Tree, batch_size: int, rounds: int):
   448→        indices = [0 for _ in range(batch_size)]
   449→        values = [random.randint(0, 2**30 - 1) for _ in range(batch_size)]
   450→        return Input(indices, values, rounds)
   451→
   452→
   453→HASH_STAGES = [
   454→    ("+", 0x7ED55D16, "+", "<<", 12),
   455→    ("^", 0xC761C23C, "^", ">>", 19),
   456→    ("+", 0x165667B1, "+", "<<", 5),
   457→    ("+", 0xD3A2646C, "^", "<<", 9),
   458→    ("+", 0xFD7046C5, "+", "<<", 3),
   459→    ("^", 0xB55A4F09, "^", ">>", 16),
   460→]
   461→
   462→
   463→def myhash(a: int) -> int:
   464→    """A simple 32-bit hash function"""
   465→    fns = {
   466→        "+": lambda x, y: x + y,
   467→        "^": lambda x, y: x ^ y,
   468→        "<<": lambda x, y: x << y,
   469→        ">>": lambda x, y: x >> y,
   470→    }
   471→
   472→    def r(x):
   473→        return x % (2**32)
   474→
   475→    for op1, val1, op2, op3, val3 in HASH_STAGES:
   476→        a = r(fns[op2](r(fns[op1](a, val1)), r(fns[op3](a, val3))))
   477→
   478→    return a
   479→
   480→
   481→def reference_kernel(t: Tree, inp: Input):
   482→    """
   483→    Reference implementation of the kernel.
   484→
   485→    A parallel tree traversal where at each node we set
   486→    cur_inp_val = myhash(cur_inp_val ^ node_val)
   487→    and then choose the left branch if cur_inp_val is even.
   488→    If we reach the bottom of the tree we wrap around to the top.
   489→    """
   490→    for h in range(inp.rounds):
   491→        for i in range(len(inp.indices)):
   492→            idx = inp.indices[i]
   493→            val = inp.values[i]
   494→            val = myhash(val ^ t.values[idx])
   495→            idx = 2 * idx + (1 if val % 2 == 0 else 2)
   496→            idx = 0 if idx >= len(t.values) else idx
   497→            inp.values[i] = val
   498→            inp.indices[i] = idx
   499→
   500→
   501→def build_mem_image(t: Tree, inp: Input) -> list[int]:
   502→    """
   503→    Build a flat memory image of the problem.
   504→    """
   505→    header = 7
   506→    extra_room = len(t.values) + len(inp.indices) * 2 + VLEN * 2 + 32
   507→    mem = [0] * (
   508→        header + len(t.values) + len(inp.indices) + len(inp.values) + extra_room
   509→    )
   510→    forest_values_p = header
   511→    inp_indices_p = forest_values_p + len(t.values)
   512→    inp_values_p = inp_indices_p + len(inp.values)
   513→    extra_room = inp_values_p + len(inp.values)
   514→
   515→    mem[0] = inp.rounds
   516→    mem[1] = len(t.values)
   517→    mem[2] = len(inp.indices)
   518→    mem[3] = t.height
   519→    mem[4] = forest_values_p
   520→    mem[5] = inp_indices_p
   521→    mem[6] = inp_values_p
   522→    mem[7] = extra_room
   523→
   524→    mem[header:inp_indices_p] = t.values
   525→    mem[inp_indices_p:inp_values_p] = inp.indices
   526→    mem[inp_values_p : inp_values_p + len(inp.values)] = inp.values
   527→    return mem
   528→
   529→
   530→def myhash_traced(a: int, trace: dict[Any, int], round: int, batch_i: int) -> int:
   531→    """A simple 32-bit hash function"""
   532→    fns = {
   533→        "+": lambda x, y: x + y,
   534→        "^": lambda x, y: x ^ y,
   535→        "<<": lambda x, y: x << y,
   536→        ">>": lambda x, y: x >> y,
   537→    }
   538→
   539→    def r(x):
   540→        return x % (2**32)
   541→
   542→    for i, (op1, val1, op2, op3, val3) in enumerate(HASH_STAGES):
   543→        a = r(fns[op2](r(fns[op1](a, val1)), r(fns[op3](a, val3))))
   544→        trace[(round, batch_i, "hash_stage", i)] = a
   545→
   546→    return a
   547→
   548→
   549→def reference_kernel2(mem: list[int], trace: dict[Any, int] = {}):
   550→    """
   551→    Reference implementation of the kernel on a flat memory.
   552→    """
   553→    # This is the initial memory layout
   554→    rounds = mem[0]
   555→    n_nodes = mem[1]
   556→    batch_size = mem[2]
   557→    forest_height = mem[3]
   558→    # Offsets into the memory which indices get added to
   559→    forest_values_p = mem[4]
   560→    inp_indices_p = mem[5]
   561→    inp_values_p = mem[6]
   562→    yield mem
   563→    for h in range(rounds):
   564→        for i in range(batch_size):
   565→            idx = mem[inp_indices_p + i]
   566→            trace[(h, i, "idx")] = idx
   567→            val = mem[inp_values_p + i]
   568→            trace[(h, i, "val")] = val
   569→            node_val = mem[forest_values_p + idx]
   570→            trace[(h, i, "node_val")] = node_val
   571→            val = myhash_traced(val ^ node_val, trace, h, i)
   572→            trace[(h, i, "hashed_val")] = val
   573→            idx = 2 * idx + (1 if val % 2 == 0 else 2)
   574→            trace[(h, i, "next_idx")] = idx
   575→            idx = 0 if idx >= n_nodes else idx
   576→            trace[(h, i, "wrapped_idx")] = idx
   577→            mem[inp_values_p + i] = val
   578→            mem[inp_indices_p + i] = idx
   579→        yield mem
   580→    # Final yield is redundant but harmless
   581→    # yield mem
   582→
```

```
     1→"""

     8→import random
     9→from copy import copy
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→from enum import Enum
    12→from typing import Any, Literal
    13→
    14→Engine = Literal["alu", "load", "store", "flow"]
    15→Instruction = dict[Engine, list[tuple]]
    16→
    17→
    18→class CoreState(Enum):
    19→    RUNNING = 1
    20→    PAUSED = 2
    21→    STOPPED = 3
    22→
    23→
    24→@dataclass
    25→class Core:
    26→    id: int
    27→    scratch: list[int]
    28→    trace_buf: list[int]
    29→    pc: int = 0
    30→    state: CoreState = CoreState.RUNNING
    31→
    32→
    33→@dataclass
    34→class DebugInfo:
    35→    """
    36→    We give you some debug info but it's up to you to use it in Machine if you
    37→    want to. You're also welcome to add more.
    38→    """
    39→
    40→    # Maps scratch variable addr to (name, len) pair
    41→    scratch_map: dict[int, (str, int)]
    42→
    43→
    44→def cdiv(a, b):
    45→    return (a + b - 1) // b
    46→
    47→
    48→SLOT_LIMITS = {
    49→    "alu": 12,
    50→    "valu": 6,
    51→    "load": 2,
    52→    "store": 2,
    53→    "flow": 1,
    54→    "debug": 64,
    55→}
    56→
    57→VLEN = 8
    58→# Older versions of the take-home used multiple cores, but this version only uses 1
    59→N_CORES = 32
    60→SCRATCH_SIZE = 1536
    61→BASE_ADDR_TID = 100000
    62→
    63→
    64→class Machine:
    65→    """
    66→    Simulator for a custom VLIW SIMD architecture.
    67→
    68→    VLIW (Very Large Instruction Word): Cores are composed of different
    69→    "engines" each of which can execute multiple "slots" per cycle in parallel.
    70→    How many slots each engine can execute per cycle is limited by SLOT_LIMITS.
    71→    Effects of instructions don't take effect until the end of cycle. Each
    72→    cycle, all engines execute all of their filled slots for that instruction.
    73→    Effects like writes to memory take place after all the inputs are read.
    74→
    75→    SIMD: There are instructions for acting on vectors of VLEN elements in a
    76→    single slot. You can use vload and vstore to load multiple contiguous
    77→    elements but not non-contiguous elements. Use vbroadcast to broadcast a
    78→    scalar to a vector and then operate on vectors with valu instructions.
    79→
    80→    The memory and scratch space are composed of 32-bit words. The solution is
    81→    plucked out of the memory at the end of the program. You can think of the
    82→    scratch space as serving the purpose of registers, constant memory, and a
    83→    manually-managed cache.
    84→
    85→    Here's an example of what an instruction might look like:
    86→
    87→    {"valu": [("*", 4, 0, 0), ("+", 8, 4, 0)], "load": [("load", 16, 17)]}
    88→
    89→    In general every number in an instruction is a scratch address except for
    90→    const and jump, and except for store and some flow instructions the first
    91→    operand is the destination.
    92→
    93→    This comment is not meant to be full ISA documentation though, for the rest
    94→    you should look through the simulator code.
    95→    """
    96→
    97→    def __init__(
    98→        self,
    99→        mem_dump: list[int],
   100→        program: list[Instruction],
   101→        debug_info: DebugInfo,
   102→        n_cores: int = 1,
   103→        scratch_size: int = SCRATCH_SIZE,
   104→        trace: bool = False,
   105→        value_trace: dict[Any, int] = {},
   106→    ):
   107→        self.cores = [
   108→            Core(id=i, scratch=[0] * scratch_size, trace_buf=[]) for i in range(n_cores)
   109→        ]
   110→        self.mem = copy(mem_dump)
   111→        self.program = program
   112→        self.debug_info = debug_info
   113→        self.value_trace = value_trace
   114→        self.prints = False
   115→        self.cycle = 0
   116→        self.enable_pause = True
   117→        self.enable_debug = True
   118→        if trace:
   119→            self.setup_trace()
   120→        else:
   121→            self.trace = None
   122→
   123→    def rewrite_instr(self, instr):
   124→        """
   125→        Rewrite an instruction to use scratch addresses instead of names
   126→        """
   127→        res = {}
   128→        for name, slots in instr.items():
   129→            res[name] = []
   130→            for slot in slots:
   131→                res[name].append(self.rewrite_slot(slot))
   132→        return res
   133→
   134→    def print_step(self, instr, core):
   135→        # print(core.id)
   136→        # print(core.trace_buf)
   137→        print(self.scratch_map(core))
   138→        print(core.pc, instr, self.rewrite_instr(instr))
   139→
   140→    def scratch_map(self, core):
   141→        res = {}
   142→        for addr, (name, length) in self.debug_info.scratch_map.items():
   143→            res[name] = core.scratch[addr : addr + length]
   144→        return res
   145→
   146→    def rewrite_slot(self, slot):
   147→        return tuple(
   148→            self.debug_info.scratch_map.get(s, (None, None))[0] or s for s in slot
   149→        )
   150→
   151→    def setup_trace(self):
   152→        """
   153→        The simulator generates traces in Chrome's Trace Event Format for
   154→        visualization in Perfetto (or chrome://tracing if you prefer it). See
   155→        the bottom of the file for info about how to use this.
   156→
   157→        See the format docs in case you want to add more info to the trace:
   158→        https://docs.google.com/document/d/REDACTED/preview
   159→        """
   160→        self.trace = open("trace.json", "w")
   161→        self.trace.write("[")
   162→        tid_counter = 0
   163→        self.tids = {}
   164→        for ci, core in enumerate(self.cores):
   165→            self.trace.write(
   166→                f'{{"name": "process_name", "ph": "M", "pid": {ci}, "tid": 0, "args": {{"name":"Core {ci}"}}}},\n'
   167→            )
   168→            for name, limit in SLOT_LIMITS.items():
   169→                if name == "debug":
   170→                    continue
   171→                for i in range(limit):
   172→                    tid_counter += 1
   173→                    self.trace.write(
   174→                        f'{{"name": "thread_name", "ph": "M", "pid": {ci}, "tid": {tid_counter}, "args": {{"name":"{name}-{i}"}}}},\n'
   175→                    )
   176→                    self.tids[(ci, name, i)] = tid_counter
   177→
   178→        # Add zero-length events at the start so all slots show up in Perfetto
   179→        for ci, core in enumerate(self.cores):
   180→            for name, limit in SLOT_LIMITS.items():
   181→                if name == "debug":
   182→                    continue
   183→                for i in range(limit):
   184→                    tid = self.tids[(ci, name, i)]
   185→                    self.trace.write(
   186→                        f'{{"name": "init", "cat": "op", "ph": "X", "pid": {ci}, "tid": {tid}, "ts": 0, "dur": 0}},\n'
   187→                    )
   188→        for ci, core in enumerate(self.cores):
   189→            self.trace.write(
   190→                f'{{"name": "process_name", "ph": "M", "pid": {len(self.cores) + ci}, "tid": 0, "args": {{"name":"Core {ci} Scratch"}}}},\n'
   191→            )
   192→            for addr, (name, length) in self.debug_info.scratch_map.items():
   193→                self.trace.write(
   194→                    f'{{"name": "thread_name", "ph": "M", "pid": {len(self.cores) + ci}, "tid": {BASE_ADDR_TID + addr}, "args": {{"name":"{name}-{length}"}}}},\n'
   195→                )
   196→
   197→    def run(self):
   198→        # 128GB RAM / 32-Thread System Optimization
   199→        # Use ThreadPool to simulate concurrent hardware execution
   200→
   201→        for core in self.cores:
   202→            if core.state == CoreState.PAUSED:
   203→                core.state = CoreState.RUNNING
   204→        while any(c.state == CoreState.RUNNING for c in self.cores):
   205→            for core in self.cores:
   206→                if core.state == CoreState.RUNNING:
   207→                    try:
   208→                        pc = core.pc
   209→                        if pc >= 0 and pc < len(self.program):
   210→                            instr = self.program[pc]
   211→                            if self.prints:
   212→                                self.print_step(instr, core)
   213→                            self.step(instr, core)
   214→                            core.pc += 1
   215→                        else:
   216→                            core.state = CoreState.STOPPED
   217→                    except IndexError:
   218→                        core.state = CoreState.STOPPED
   219→                    except Exception as e:
   220→                        print(f"Core {core.id} Crash: {e}")
   221→                        core.state = CoreState.STOPPED
   222→            self.cycle += 1
   223→
   224→        # Dump trace on finish
   225→        for core in self.cores:
   226→            print(f"TRACE CORE {core.id}: {core.trace_buf}")
   227→
   228→    def alu(self, core, op, dest, a1, a2):
   229→        a1 = core.scratch[a1]
   230→        a2 = core.scratch[a2]
   231→        match op:
   232→            case "+":
   233→                res = a1 + a2
   234→            case "-":
   235→                res = a1 - a2
   236→            case "*":
   237→                res = a1 * a2
   238→            case "//":
   239→                res = a1 // a2
   240→            case "cdiv":
   241→                res = cdiv(a1, a2)
   242→            case "^":
   243→                res = a1 ^ a2
   244→            case "&":
   245→                res = a1 & a2
   246→            case "|":
   247→                res = a1 | a2
   248→            case "<<":
   249→                res = a1 << a2
   250→            case ">>":
   251→                res = a1 >> a2
   252→            case "%":
   253→                res = a1 % a2
   254→            case "<":
   255→                res = int(a1 < a2)
   256→            case "==":
   257→                res = int(a1 == a2)
   258→            case _:
   259→                raise NotImplementedError(f"Unknown alu op {op}")
   260→        res = res % (2**32)
   261→        self.scratch_write[dest] = res
   262→
   263→    def valu(self, core, *slot):
   264→        match slot:
   265→            case ("vbroadcast", dest, src):
   266→                for i in range(VLEN):
   267→                    self.scratch_write[dest + i] = core.scratch[src]
   268→            case ("multiply_add", dest, a, b, c):
   269→                for i in range(VLEN):
   270→                    mul = (core.scratch[a + i] * core.scratch[b + i]) % (2**32)
   271→                    self.scratch_write[dest + i] = (mul + core.scratch[c + i]) % (2**32)
   272→            case (op, dest, a1, a2):
   273→                for i in range(VLEN):
   274→                    self.alu(core, op, dest + i, a1 + i, a2 + i)
   275→            case _:
   276→                raise NotImplementedError(f"Unknown valu op {slot}")
   277→
   278→    def load(self, core, *slot):
   279→        match slot:
   280→            case ("load", dest, addr):
   281→                # print(dest, addr, core.scratch[addr])
   282→                try:
   283→                    self.scratch_write[dest] = self.mem[core.scratch[addr]]
   284→                except IndexError:
   285→                    print(
   286→                        f"ERROR: load out of bounds. cycle={self.cycle} core={core.id} addr_reg={addr} val={core.scratch[addr]} len(mem)={len(self.mem)}"
   287→                    )
   288→                    raise
   289→            case ("load_offset", dest, addr, offset):
   290→                # Handy for treating vector dest and addr as a full block in the mini-compiler if you want
   291→                self.scratch_write[dest + offset] = self.mem[
   292→                    core.scratch[addr + offset]
   293→                ]
   294→            case ("vload", dest, addr):  # addr is a scalar
   295→                addr = core.scratch[addr]
   296→                for vi in range(VLEN):
   297→                    try:
   298→                        self.scratch_write[dest + vi] = self.mem[addr + vi]
   299→                    except IndexError:
   300→                        print(
   301→                            f"ERROR: vload out of bounds. cycle={self.cycle} core={core.id} addr={addr} vi={vi} len(mem)={len(self.mem)}"
   302→                        )
   303→                        raise
   304→            case ("const", dest, val):
   305→                self.scratch_write[dest] = (val) % (2**32)
   306→            case _:
   307→                raise NotImplementedError(f"Unknown load op {slot}")
   308→
   309→    def store(self, core, *slot):
   310→        match slot:
   311→            case ("store", addr, src):
   312→                addr = core.scratch[addr]
   313→                self.mem_write[addr] = core.scratch[src]
   314→            case ("vstore", addr, src):  # addr is a scalar
   315→                addr = core.scratch[addr]
   316→                for vi in range(VLEN):
   317→                    self.mem_write[addr + vi] = core.scratch[src + vi]
   318→            case _:
   319→                raise NotImplementedError(f"Unknown store op {slot}")
   320→
   321→    def flow(self, core, *slot):
   322→        match slot:
   323→            case ("select", dest, cond, a, b):
   324→                self.scratch_write[dest] = (
   325→                    core.scratch[a] if core.scratch[cond] != 0 else core.scratch[b]
   326→                )
   327→            case ("add_imm", dest, a, imm):
   328→                self.scratch_write[dest] = (core.scratch[a] + imm) % (2**32)
   329→            case ("vselect", dest, cond, a, b):
   330→                for vi in range(VLEN):
   331→                    self.scratch_write[dest + vi] = (
   332→                        core.scratch[a + vi]
   333→                        if core.scratch[cond + vi] != 0
   334→                        else core.scratch[b + vi]
   335→                    )
   336→            case ("halt",):
   337→                core.state = CoreState.STOPPED
   338→            case ("pause",):
   339→                if self.enable_pause:
   340→                    core.state = CoreState.PAUSED
   341→            case ("trace_write", val):
   342→                core.trace_buf.append(core.scratch[val])
   343→            case ("cond_jump", cond, addr):
   344→                if core.scratch[cond] != 0:
   345→                    core.pc = addr
   346→            case ("cond_jump_rel", cond, offset):
   347→                if core.scratch[cond] != 0:
   348→                    core.pc += offset
   349→            case ("jump", addr):
   350→                core.pc = addr
   351→            case ("jump_indirect", addr):
   352→                core.pc = core.scratch[addr]
   353→            case ("coreid", dest):
   354→                self.scratch_write[dest] = core.id
   355→            case _:
   356→                raise NotImplementedError(f"Unknown flow op {slot}")
   357→
   358→    def trace_post_step(self, instr, core):
   359→        # You can add extra stuff to the trace if you want!
   360→        for addr, (name, length) in self.debug_info.scratch_map.items():
   361→            if any((addr + vi) in self.scratch_write for vi in range(length)):
   362→                val = str(core.scratch[addr : addr + length])
   363→                val = val.replace("[", "").replace("]", "")
   364→                self.trace.write(
   365→                    f'{{"name": "{val}", "cat": "op", "ph": "X", "pid": {len(self.cores) + core.id}, "tid": {BASE_ADDR_TID + addr}, "ts": {self.cycle}, "dur": 1 }},\n'
   366→                )
   367→
   368→    def trace_slot(self, core, slot, name, i):
   369→        self.trace.write(
   370→            f'{{"name": "{slot[0]}", "cat": "op", "ph": "X", "pid": {core.id}, "tid": {self.tids[(core.id, name, i)]}, "ts": {self.cycle}, "dur": 1, "args":{{"slot": "{str(slot)}", "named": "{str(self.rewrite_slot(slot))}" }} }},\n'
   371→        )
   372→
   373→    def step(self, instr: Instruction, core):
   374→        """
   375→        Execute all the slots in each engine for a single instruction bundle
   376→        """
   377→        ENGINE_FNS = {
   378→            "alu": self.alu,
   379→            "valu": self.valu,
   380→            "load": self.load,
   381→            "store": self.store,
   382→            "flow": self.flow,
   383→            "debug": lambda core, *slot: (
   384→                core.trace_buf.append(core.scratch[slot[1]])
   385→                if slot[0] == "trace"
   386→                else None
   387→            ),
   388→        }
   389→        self.scratch_write = {}
   390→        self.mem_write = {}
   391→        for name, slots in instr.items():
   392→            if name == "debug":
   393→                if not self.enable_debug:
   394→                    continue
   395→                for slot in slots:
   396→                    if slot[0] == "compare":
   397→                        loc, key = slot[1], slot[2]
   398→                        ref = self.value_trace[key]
   399→                        res = core.scratch[loc]
   400→                        assert res == ref, f"{res} != {ref} for {key} at pc={core.pc}"
   401→                    elif slot[0] == "vcompare":
   402→                        loc, keys = slot[1], slot[2]
   403→                        ref = [self.value_trace[key] for key in keys]
   404→                        res = core.scratch[loc : loc + VLEN]
   405→                        assert res == ref, (
   406→                            f"{res} != {ref} for {keys} at pc={core.pc} loc={loc}"
   407→                        )
   408→                continue
   409→            assert len(slots) <= SLOT_LIMITS[name]
   410→            for i, slot in enumerate(slots):
   411→                if self.trace is not None:
   412→                    self.trace_slot(core, slot, name, i)
   413→                ENGINE_FNS[name](core, *slot)
   414→        for addr, val in self.scratch_write.items():
   415→            core.scratch[addr] = val
   416→        for addr, val in self.mem_write.items():
   417→            self.mem[addr] = val
   418→
   419→        if self.trace:
   420→            self.trace_post_step(instr, core)
   421→
   422→        del self.scratch_write
   423→        del self.mem_write
   424→
   425→    def __del__(self):
   426→        if self.trace is not None:
   427→            self.trace.write("]")
   428→            self.trace.close()
   429→
   430→
   431→@dataclass
   432→class Tree:
   433→    """
   434→    An implicit perfect balanced binary tree with values on the nodes.
   435→    """
   436→
   437→    height: int
   438→    values: list[int]
   439→
   440→    @staticmethod
   441→    def generate(height: int):
   442→        n_nodes = 2 ** (height + 1) - 1
   443→        values = [random.randint(0, 2**30 - 1) for _ in range(n_nodes)]
   444→        return Tree(height, values)
   445→
   446→
   447→@dataclass
   448→class Input:
   449→    """
   450→    A batch of inputs, indices to nodes (starting as 0) and initial input
   451→    values. We then iterate these for a specified number of rounds.
   452→    """
   453→
   454→    indices: list[int]
   455→    values: list[int]
   456→    rounds: int
   457→
   458→    @staticmethod
   459→    def generate(forest: Tree, batch_size: int, rounds: int):
   460→        indices = [0 for _ in range(batch_size)]
   461→        values = [random.randint(0, 2**30 - 1) for _ in range(batch_size)]
   462→        return Input(indices, values, rounds)
   463→
   464→
   465→HASH_STAGES = [
   466→    ("+", 0x7ED55D16, "+", "<<", 12),
   467→    ("^", 0xC761C23C, "^", ">>", 19),
   468→    ("+", 0x165667B1, "+", "<<", 5),
   469→    ("+", 0xD3A2646C, "^", "<<", 9),
   470→    ("+", 0xFD7046C5, "+", "<<", 3),
   471→    ("^", 0xB55A4F09, "^", ">>", 16),
   472→]
   473→
   474→
   475→def myhash(a: int) -> int:
   476→    """A simple 32-bit hash function"""
   477→    fns = {
   478→        "+": lambda x, y: x + y,
   479→        "^": lambda x, y: x ^ y,
   480→        "<<": lambda x, y: x << y,
   481→        ">>": lambda x, y: x >> y,
   482→    }
   483→
   484→    def r(x):
   485→        return x % (2**32)
   486→
   487→    for op1, val1, op2, op3, val3 in HASH_STAGES:
   488→        a = r(fns[op2](r(fns[op1](a, val1)), r(fns[op3](a, val3))))
   489→
   490→    return a
   491→
   492→
   493→def reference_kernel(t: Tree, inp: Input):
   494→    """
   495→    Reference implementation of the kernel.
   496→
   497→    A parallel tree traversal where at each node we set
   498→    cur_inp_val = myhash(cur_inp_val ^ node_val)
   499→    and then choose the left branch if cur_inp_val is even.
   500→    If we reach the bottom of the tree we wrap around to the top.
   501→    """
   502→    for h in range(inp.rounds):
   503→        for i in range(len(inp.indices)):
   504→            idx = inp.indices[i]
   505→            val = inp.values[i]
   506→            val = myhash(val ^ t.values[idx])
   507→            idx = 2 * idx + (1 if val % 2 == 0 else 2)
   508→            idx = 0 if idx >= len(t.values) else idx
   509→            inp.values[i] = val
   510→            inp.indices[i] = idx
   511→
   512→
   513→def build_mem_image(t: Tree, inp: Input) -> list[int]:
   514→    """
   515→    Build a flat memory image of the problem.
   516→    """
   517→    header = 7
   518→    extra_room = len(t.values) + len(inp.indices) * 2 + VLEN * 2 + 32
   519→    mem = [0] * (
   520→        header + len(t.values) + len(inp.indices) + len(inp.values) + extra_room
   521→    )
   522→    forest_values_p = header
   523→    inp_indices_p = forest_values_p + len(t.values)
   524→    inp_values_p = inp_indices_p + len(inp.values)
   525→    extra_room = inp_values_p + len(inp.values)
   526→
   527→    mem[0] = inp.rounds
   528→    mem[1] = len(t.values)
   529→    mem[2] = len(inp.indices)
   530→    mem[3] = t.height
   531→    mem[4] = forest_values_p
   532→    mem[5] = inp_indices_p
   533→    mem[6] = inp_values_p
   534→    mem[7] = extra_room
   535→
   536→    mem[header:inp_indices_p] = t.values
   537→    mem[inp_indices_p:inp_values_p] = inp.indices
   538→    mem[inp_values_p : inp_values_p + len(inp.values)] = inp.values
   539→    return mem
   540→
   541→
   542→def myhash_traced(a: int, trace: dict[Any, int], round: int, batch_i: int) -> int:
   543→    """A simple 32-bit hash function"""
   544→    fns = {
   545→        "+": lambda x, y: x + y,
   546→        "^": lambda x, y: x ^ y,
   547→        "<<": lambda x, y: x << y,
   548→        ">>": lambda x, y: x >> y,
   549→    }
   550→
   551→    def r(x):
   552→        return x % (2**32)
   553→
   554→    for i, (op1, val1, op2, op3, val3) in enumerate(HASH_STAGES):
   555→        a = r(fns[op2](r(fns[op1](a, val1)), r(fns[op3](a, val3))))
   556→        trace[(round, batch_i, "hash_stage", i)] = a
   557→
   558→    return a
   559→
   560→
   561→def reference_kernel2(mem: list[int], trace: dict[Any, int] = {}):
   562→    """
   563→    Reference implementation of the kernel on a flat memory.
   564→    """
   565→    # This is the initial memory layout
   566→    rounds = mem[0]
   567→    n_nodes = mem[1]
   568→    batch_size = mem[2]
   569→    forest_height = mem[3]
   570→    # Offsets into the memory which indices get added to
   571→    forest_values_p = mem[4]
   572→    inp_indices_p = mem[5]
   573→    inp_values_p = mem[6]
   574→    yield mem
   575→    for h in range(rounds):
   576→        for i in range(batch_size):
   577→            idx = mem[inp_indices_p + i]
   578→            trace[(h, i, "idx")] = idx
   579→            val = mem[inp_values_p + i]
   580→            trace[(h, i, "val")] = val
   581→            node_val = mem[forest_values_p + idx]
   582→            trace[(h, i, "node_val")] = node_val
   583→            val = myhash_traced(val ^ node_val, trace, h, i)
   584→            trace[(h, i, "hashed_val")] = val
   585→            idx = 2 * idx + (1 if val % 2 == 0 else 2)
   586→            trace[(h, i, "next_idx")] = idx
   587→            idx = 0 if idx >= n_nodes else idx
   588→            trace[(h, i, "wrapped_idx")] = idx
   589→            mem[inp_values_p + i] = val
   590→            mem[inp_indices_p + i] = idx
   591→        yield mem
   592→    # Final yield is redundant but harmless
   593→    # yield mem
   594→
```

```
     9→# Configuration

    11→MODELS = {
    12→    "Architect": "deepseek-r1:70b",  # Reasoning Expert
    13→    "Engineer": "qwen3-coder:30b",  # Coding Expert
    14→    "Critic": "mistral:7b",  # Fast Critic (if available, otherwise fallback)

    16→
    17→# Fallback if models not present (we'll detect from available_models.json if needed, but hardcoding intent for now)
    18→# Realistically, we'd read the available_models.json, but let's assume the user's roster is accurate.
    19→
    20→INITIAL_PROMPT = """
    21→We are optimizing a VLIW kernel for random tree traversal hashing on a custom simulator.
    22→Goal: Minimize cycles. Current barrier: 2048 cycles (Load Bound). Target: <1487 cycles.
    23→Machine: 1 Core, VLIW, SIMD (VLEN=8).
    24→Constraints:
    25→- 2 Load slots per cycle.
    26→- 12 ALU slots.
    27→- 6 VALU slots.
    28→- Latency: Loads are effective next cycle.
    29→- Problem: Random Tree Traversal `node_val = memory[idx]`. `idx = 2*idx + hash(val ^ node_val)`.
    30→- Dependency: `idx` depends on `node_val`, so we can't issue next load until current is done.
    31→- "Smart Load" approach (loading operands into registers and using ALU to select) helps but hits ALU limits.
    32→
    33→Objective: Brainstorm NOVEL, "Outside the Box" strategies using FLUME (Fluid Latent Understanding), HIHO (Half-In Half-Out), and MANIFOLD theory.
    34→Think about:
    35→1. **Reality Distortion**: Can we exploit the simulator's `SLOT_LIMITS` or execution order?
    36→2. **Predicate Speculation**: Can we speculate both branches?
    37→3. **Manifold Alignment**: Is the "random" hash actually residing on a lower-dimensional manifold we can predict?
    38→4. **Compression**: Can we pack data?
    39→5. **Hardware**: How to use 32 cores for the search?
    40→
    41→Start the discussion.
    42→"""
    43→
    44→
    45→def query_ollama(model, prompt, context=None):
    46→    logging.info(f"Querying {model}...")
    47→    try:
    48→        response = requests.post(
    49→            OLLAMA_API,
    50→            json={
    51→                "model": model,
    52→                "prompt": prompt,
    53→                "context": context,
    54→                "stream": False,
    55→            },
    56→            timeout=120,
    57→        )
    58→        response.raise_for_status()
    59→        return response.json()
    60→    except Exception as e:
    61→        logging.error(f"Failed to query {model}: {e}")
    62→        return {"response": f"[Error: {e}]", "context": context}
    63→
    64→
    65→def run_roundtable():
    66→    history = INITIAL_PROMPT
    67→    context = None
    68→
    69→    rounds = 2
    70→    conversation = []
    71→
    72→    # Round 1: Architect (DeepSeek)
    73→    print(">>> ARCHITECT (DeepSeek-R1) IS THINKING...")
    74→    res = query_ollama(MODELS["Architect"], history, context)
    75→    response_text = res.get("response", "")
    76→    context = res.get("context")
    77→    conversation.append(f"**ARCHITECT**: {response_text}\n")
    78→    history += f"\n\nArchitect: {response_text}"
    79→    print(response_text)
    80→
    81→    # Round 2: Engineer (Qwen)
    82→    print("\n>>> ENGINEER (Qwen3-Coder) IS CRITIQUING & PROPOSING...")
    83→    prompt = f"Critique the Architect's ideas and propose concrete VLIW implementation details. \nContext:\n{history}"
    84→    res = query_ollama(
    85→        MODELS["Engineer"], prompt, context
    86→    )  # Pass context to keep memory? Actually prompt includes history.
    87→    response_text = res.get("response", "")
    88→    context = res.get("context")
    89→    conversation.append(f"**ENGINEER**: {response_text}\n")
    90→    history += f"\n\nEngineer: {response_text}"
    91→    print(response_text)
    92→
    93→    # Round 3: Statistician/Critic (Mistral - using Architect again for synthesis if Mistral weak)
    94→    print("\n>>> SYNTHESIZER (DeepSeek-R1) IS SUMMARIZING...")
    95→    prompt = f"Synthesize a concrete Action Plan strategies. Focus on 'Reality Distortion' (finding simulator bugs) and 'Swarm Search'. \nContext:\n{history}"
    96→    res = query_ollama(MODELS["Architect"], prompt, context)
    97→    response_text = res.get("response", "")
    98→    conversation.append(f"**SYNTHESIS**: {response_text}\n")
    99→    print(response_text)
   100→
   101→    # Save artifact
   102→    with open("swarm_brainstorm_result.md", "w") as f:
   103→        f.write("# Cohezion Swarm Roundtable: VLIW Optimization\n\n")
   104→        f.write("\n".join(conversation))
   105→
   106→
   107→if __name__ == "__main__":
   108→    run_roundtable()
   109→
```

```
    64→class Machine:
    65→    """
    66→    Simulator for a custom VLIW SIMD architecture.
    67→
    68→    VLIW (Very Large Instruction Word): Cores are composed of different
    69→    "engines" each of which can execute multiple "slots" per cycle in parallel.
    70→    How many slots each engine can execute per cycle is limited by SLOT_LIMITS.
    71→    Effects of instructions don't take effect until the end of cycle. Each
    72→    cycle, all engines execute all of their filled slots for that instruction.
    73→    Effects like writes to memory take place after all the inputs are read.
    74→
    75→    SIMD: There are instructions for acting on vectors of VLEN elements in a
    76→    single slot. You can use vload and vstore to load multiple contiguous
    77→    elements but not non-contiguous elements. Use vbroadcast to broadcast a
    78→    scalar to a vector and then operate on vectors with valu instructions.
    79→
    80→    The memory and scratch space are composed of 32-bit words. The solution is
```
